I'll pick g2g1.

Wait, let me double check the Queen at h4.
h4 is (4,7).
Diagonal: (3,6) g5, (2,5) f6, (1,4) e7, (0,3) d8.
Diagonal: (5,6) g3, (6,5) f2, (7,4) e1.
So if King moves to f1 (7,5), is it attacked by the Queen?
h4(4,7) -> f2(6,5) -> e1(7,4).
f1(7,5) is NOT on the diagonal h4-e1.
Wait, let me check (4,7) to (7,4).
Delta row = 7-4 = 3.
Delta col = 4-7 = -3.
Yes, the diagonal is (4,7), (5,6), (6,5), (7,4).
f1 is (7,5).
So f1 is not on that diagonal.

Is g1 (7,6) attacked?
- Rook a2(6,0): no.
- Queen h4(4,7): column h is col 7, row 4. (7,6) is row 7, col 6. Nope.

Both seem equally safe for one move. I will choose g2g1.
